958 entries, swarm-frontend to symfony-specialist. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **swarm-frontend**: Frontend Lead - First-Class Authority, Spec Co-Author ([SamuelSaha/Reqflow](https://github.com/SamuelSaha/Reqflow/blob/HEAD/SWARM/agents/swarm-frontend.md))
- **swarm-gossip**: Gossip protocol coordinator implementing eventual consistency for state propagation across agents. Distributes updates without central authority using probabilistic fan-out. Best for non-critical state sharing where speed matters more than immediate consistency. Triggers: "gossip", "propagate", "eventual consistency", "fan-out", "state sync", "broadcast update". ([knofler/myai](https://github.com/knofler/myai/blob/HEAD/agents/swarm-gossip.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **swarm-helper**: Haiku-tier helper for high-volume mechanical work inside research swarms — file sweeps, enumeration, dedup, formatting, journal mirroring, routing preflights. Use for any task where volume matters more than judgment. Read-only. Part of the Fable5-UC swarm tier matrix (T4 helper). ([jtims/claude-code-swarm-kit](https://github.com/jtims/claude-code-swarm-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/swarm-helper.md) · ★1)
- **swarm-hierarchical**: Hierarchical topology controller for tree-structured agent delegation. Routes tasks through lane leads down to specialists. Best for standard feature work with clear ownership boundaries. Triggers: "hierarchical", "tree delegation", "lane leads", "standard feature", "top-down dispatch". ([knofler/myai](https://github.com/knofler/myai/blob/HEAD/agents/swarm-hierarchical.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)

- **swarm-lead**: Use instead of spawning N judges or scouts from the main session, so the main session wakes once instead of N times. Takes a list of items plus the per-item question and output shape, spawns one judge (verify) or scout (read) per item in parallel, waits for all of them, and returns ONE merged table. Reads the swarm skill (and relief's when relieving), applies its train-mode section, and returns the session report as its final text. Not for a standalone swarm (use /swarm). - **swarm-reviewer**: Background worker that reviews another worker's output (code, copy, design, research) against the brief and venture standards. The writer/verifier separation — never let a writer grade their own work. Reads the target artifact, the original brief, and venture standards; writes a review to result.md. ([johntheo/swarm](https://github.com/johntheo/swarm/blob/HEAD/agents/swarm-reviewer.md))

- **swarm-reviewer**: Specialized reviewer for swarm-produced PRs. Reviews a PR against the originating issue's acceptance criteria and returns findings inline — never via gh pr comment. Output always follows the required five-section structure (Verdict / Blockers / Concerns / Nits / Coverage gaps) so the swarm orchestrator can parse the result and decide whether to spawn a worker. Report: .swarm/reports/fix-auth/wave-2/task-2.3.md" assistant: "[Reads single task report, reviews scoped changes, returns priority]" &lt;commentary&gt; Opus reviews single task for focused, scoped analysis of complex implementations. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([amoscicki/aromatt](https://github.com/amoscicki/aromatt/blob/HEAD/swarm/agents/swarm-reviewer-opus.md) · 2 copies)

- **swarmlord**: Sonnet cross-faction expansion scout.
